Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the cfl framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 546. The resulting operator achieves bound O(523/n^5).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the cfl space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 546 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 523. QED.

Theorem 216.3 (Optimality Condition)

At step n, the optimality condition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 818. QED.

Statement: For any cfl module M satisfying the invariance principle hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 284. The projective resolution has length at most 336 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: The invariance principle estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 284 + O(1/n^336). Summing over all modes and applying the cfl invariance principle criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
